# ArcForges implementation tasks — remote edition

Use one task block at a time with [the remote execution profile](https://github.com/ArcForges/Plan/blob/main/arcforges-implementation-remote.md). Each block explicitly selects its task and inherits the complete collection, implementation, review, CI, merge and post-merge rules from that profile. It replaces the profile's Current task for that run; do not also execute the default task or subsequent blocks.

This edition preserves all 447 task IDs, their order, scopes and dependencies from [list.md](https://github.com/ArcForges/Plan/blob/main/list.md). All formal-document entry points are GitHub URLs. Resolve the current Design baseline once per run and read a coherent commit as instructed by the profile.
